E.1.
Proof of Lemma
10.3

This follows calculations with the definition (
10.1
) of
\bar{T}_{n}
:

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{n}\big(\bm{X},\bm{Y}\big)=\dcov^{2}(X,Y)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\tau_{X}\tau_{Y}n}\sum_{i}\Big(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i}-\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}-\frac{\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}}{2\tau_{X}^{2}}(\lVert X_{i}\rVert^{2}-\tr(\Sigma_{X}))-\frac{\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}}{2\tau_{Y}^{2}}(\lVert Y_{i}\rVert^{2}-\tr(\Sigma_{Y}))\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\tau_{X}\tau_{Y}\cdot 2\binom{n}{2}}\sum_{I_{n}^{2}}\bigg[\Big(X_{i_{1}}^{\top}X_{i_{2}}Y_{i_{1}}^{\top}Y_{i_{2}}-X_{i_{1}}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i_{1}}-X_{i_{2}}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i_{2}}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\Big(X_{i_{1}}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i_{2}}+X_{i_{2}}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i_{1}}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\dcov^{2}(X,Y)-\frac{2}{\tau_{X}\tau_{Y}n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg[\frac{\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}}{2\tau_{X}^{2}}\big(\lVert X_{i}\rVert^{2}-\tr(\Sigma_{X})\big)+\frac{\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}}{2\tau_{Y}^{2}}\big(\lVert Y_{i}\rVert^{2}-\tr(\Sigma_{Y})\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\tau_{X}\tau_{Y}\cdot 2\binom{n}{2}}\sum_{I_{n}^{2}}\bigg[\Big(X_{i_{1}}^{\top}X_{i_{2}}Y_{i_{1}}^{\top}Y_{i_{2}}-\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\Big)-\Big(X_{i_{1}}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i_{2}}+X_{i_{2}}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i_{1}}\Big)\bigg].

The claim now follows by the definitions of
\psi_{1}
-
\psi_{3}
.∎

E.2.
Proof of Proposition
10.4

E.2.1.
Variance bound

Proof of Proposition
10.4
-(1).

With
W_{i}\equiv X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{i}\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X},\bm{Y}\big)=\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X},\bm{Y}\big)-\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X}^{\{i\}},\bm{Y}^{\{i\}}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\neq i_{2},\\
i_{1},i_{2}\neq i\end{subarray}}\tr\big(W_{i_{1}}W_{i_{2}}^{\top}\big)+2\sum_{j\neq i}\tr(W_{i}W_{j}^{\top})-\bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\neq i_{2},\\
i_{1},i_{2}\neq i\end{subarray}}\tr\big(W_{i_{1}}W_{i_{2}}^{\top}\big)+2\sum_{j\neq i}\tr(W_{i}^{\prime}W_{j}^{\top})\bigg]

(E.1)

\displaystyle=2\tr\bigg[\big(W_{i}-W_{i}^{\prime}\big)\sum_{j\neq i}W_{j}^{\top}\bigg]=2\bigg[Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}-Y_{i}^{\prime\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}^{\prime}\bigg].

So for any
A\subset\{1,\ldots,n\}
and
i\notin A
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{i}\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X}^{A},\bm{Y}^{A}\big)=2\tr\bigg[\big(W_{i}-W_{i}^{\prime}\big)\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}W_{j}+\sum_{j\in A}W_{j}^{\prime}\bigg)^{\top}\bigg]

\displaystyle=2\bigg[Y_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+\sum_{j\in A}Y_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}^{\prime\top}\bigg)X_{i}-Y_{i}^{\prime\top}\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+\sum_{j\in A}Y_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}^{\prime\top}\bigg)X_{i}^{\prime}\bigg].

Then with
\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\equiv\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}^{\prime},\bm{Y}^{\prime}}
, we have

\displaystyle 4^{-1}\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\big[\Delta_{i}\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X},\bm{Y}\big)\Delta_{i}\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X}^{A},\bm{Y}^{A}\big)\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\bigg[Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}\cdot Y_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+\sum_{j\in A}Y_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}^{\prime\top}\bigg)X_{i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\bigg[Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}\cdot Y_{i}^{\prime\top}\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+\sum_{j\in A}Y_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}^{\prime\top}\bigg)X_{i}^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\bigg[Y_{i}^{\prime\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}^{\prime}\cdot Y_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+\sum_{j\in A}Y_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}^{\prime\top}\bigg)X_{i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\bigg[Y_{i}^{\prime\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}^{\prime}\cdot Y_{i}^{\prime\top}\bigg(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+\sum_{j\in A}Y_{j}^{\prime}X_{j}^{\prime\top}\bigg)X_{i}^{\prime}\bigg]

\displaystyle\equiv B_{1}(i,A)-B_{2}(i,A)-B_{3}(i,A)+B_{4}(i,A).

The four terms can be calculated as follows:

•

By direct calculation,

\displaystyle B_{1}(i,A)

\displaystyle=Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}\cdot Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{YX}\Big)X_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{i}^{\top}X_{j})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{j})(X_{i}^{\top}X_{k})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{k})+|A|(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i})\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}(X_{i}^{\top}X_{j})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{j}).

•

Using independence between
(X_{i}^{\prime},Y_{i}^{\prime})
and
\{(X_{j}^{\prime},Y_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in A}
as
i\notin A
,

\displaystyle B_{2}(i,A)

\displaystyle=Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big)X_{i}\cdot\tr\Big[\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{YX}\Big)\Sigma_{XY}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{i}^{\top}X_{j})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{j})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})+|A|\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}(X_{i}^{\top}X_{j})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{j}).

•

Again by independence between
(X_{i}^{\prime},Y_{i}^{\prime})
and
\{(X_{j}^{\prime},Y_{j}^{\prime})\}_{j\in A}
as
i\notin A
,

\displaystyle B_{3}(i,A)

\displaystyle=\tr\Big(\Big[\sum_{j\neq i}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}\Big]\Sigma_{XY}\Big)\cdot Y_{i}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{YX}\Big)X_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})(X_{i}^{\top}X_{k})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{k})+|A|(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i})\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j}.

•

By Lemma
G.4
-(4), we have

\displaystyle B_{4}(i,A)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\Big[X_{i}^{\prime\top}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}\Big)Y_{i}^{\prime}\cdot X_{i}^{\prime\top}\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{XY}\Big)Y_{i}^{\prime}\Big]

\displaystyle=\tr\Big[\Sigma_{X}\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}\Big)\Sigma_{Y}\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}Y_{j}X_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{YX}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\tr\Big[\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}\Big)\Sigma_{YX}\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{XY}\Big)\Sigma_{YX}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\tr\Big[\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}\Big)\Sigma_{YX}\Big]\cdot\tr\Big[\Big(\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{XY}\Big)\Sigma_{YX}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa\tr\big(M_{1}(i,A)\circ M_{2}(i,A)\big)

\displaystyle=\Big[\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})+|A|\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\Big[\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})+|A|\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\Big[\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})+|A|\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa\tr\big(M_{1}(i,A)\circ M_{2}(i,A)\big).

Here
M_{1}(i,A)\equiv(M_{1}(i,A))(\bm{X},\bm{Y})\equiv\Sigma^{1/2}[0,\bar{M}_{1}(i,A);0,0]\Sigma^{1/2}
and
M_{2}(i,A)\equiv(M_{2}(i,A))(\bm{X},\bm{Y})\equiv\Sigma^{1/2}[0,\bar{M}_{2}(i,A);0,0]\Sigma^{1/2}
, with
\bar{M}_{1}(i,A)\equiv\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}
and
\bar{M}_{2}(i,A)\equiv\sum_{j\notin A\cup\{i\}}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}+|A|\cdot\Sigma_{XY}
.

Hence we have

\displaystyle B_{1}(i,A)-B_{2}(i,A)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{i}^{\top}X_{j})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{j})\big[X_{k}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{k}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+|A|\cdot\big(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i}-\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\big)\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}(X_{i}^{\top}X_{j})(Y_{i}^{\top}Y_{j}),

and, by combining
-B_{3}(i,A)
with the last term of
B_{4}(i,A)
,

\displaystyle B_{4}(i,A)-B_{3}(i,A)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})+|A|\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})+|A|\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})\big[X_{k}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{k}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-|A|\cdot(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i}-\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j}+\kappa\tr\big(M_{1}(i,A)\circ M_{2}(i,A)\big).

This implies that

\displaystyle 4^{-1}\mathbb{E}^{\prime}\big[\Delta_{i}\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X},\bm{Y}\big)\Delta_{i}\psi_{1}\big(\bm{X}^{A},\bm{Y}^{A}\big)\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})+\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}\big[X_{j}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{j}\big]\big[X_{k}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{k}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+|A|\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j}+|A|\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+|A|\cdot(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i}-\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\cdot\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{j}+\kappa\tr\big(M_{1}(i,A)\circ M_{2}(i,A)\big)

\displaystyle\equiv 4^{-1}\cdot\sum_{\ell=1}^{7}T_{\ell}(i,A).

By definition, we have

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{2}\sum_{A\subsetneq\{1,\ldots,n\}}\sum_{i\notin A}\frac{T_{1}(i,A)}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{A\subsetneq\{1,\ldots,n\}}\sum_{i\notin A}\sum_{j\neq i,k\notin A\cup\{i\}}\frac{(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq n,\\
j\neq i,k\neq i\end{subarray}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})\cdot\bigg[\sum_{A\cap\{i,k\}=\emptyset}\frac{1}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\ast)}}{{=}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq n,\\
j\neq i,k\neq i\end{subarray}}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})=(n-2)\sum_{j,k}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})+\sum_{j=1}^{n}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{j})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j}).

Here in (
\ast
) we used

(E.2)

\displaystyle\sum_{A\cap\{i,k\}=\emptyset}\frac{1}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{a=0}^{n-2}\sum_{A\cap\{i,k\}=\emptyset,\lvert A\rvert=a}\frac{1}{\binom{n}{a}(n-a)}=\sum_{a=0}^{n-2}\frac{\binom{n-2}{a}}{\binom{n}{a}(n-a)}=\sum_{a=0}^{n-2}\frac{n-a-1}{n(n-1)}=\frac{1}{2}.

With completely analogous definitions and calculations, we have

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=(n-2)\sum_{j,k}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})+\sum_{j=1}^{n}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})^{2},

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg[\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{j}\bigg]^{2}.

Next, we have

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{2}\sum_{A\subsetneq\{1,\ldots,n\}}\sum_{i\notin A}\frac{T_{4}(i,A)}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{A\subsetneq\{1,\ldots,n\}}\sum_{i\notin A}\sum_{j\neq i}\frac{|A|\cdot(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j})}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{1\leq i\leq n,j\neq i}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j})\cdot\bigg[\sum_{A:i\notin A}\frac{|A|}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\ast\ast)}}{{=}}(n-1)\cdot\sum_{1\leq i\leq n,j\neq i}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j}=(n-1)^{2}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{j}.

Here in
(**)
we used

\displaystyle\sum_{A:i\notin A}\frac{|A|}{\binom{n}{\lvert A\rvert}(n-\lvert A\rvert)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{a=0}^{n-1}\sum_{A:i\notin A,\lvert A\rvert=a}\frac{a}{\binom{n}{a}(n-a)}=\sum_{a=0}^{n-1}\frac{a\binom{n-1}{a}}{\binom{n}{a}(n-a)}=\sum_{a=0}^{n-1}\frac{a}{n}=\frac{n-1}{2}.

With completely analogous definitions and calculations, we have

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle=(n-1)^{2}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j},

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle=(n-1)\cdot\sum_{1\leq i\leq n,j\neq i}(X_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{i}-\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\cdot\big[X_{j}^{\top}(X_{i}Y_{i}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{j}\big],

\displaystyle T_{7}

\displaystyle=\kappa\cdot\Big[(n-1)^{2}\tr\Big(\Sigma^{1/2}\begin{pmatrix}0&\sum_{j=1}^{n}X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}\Sigma^{1/2}\circ G_{[12]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{j,k}(n-2+\bm{1}_{j=k})\tr\Big(\Sigma^{1/2}\begin{pmatrix}0&X_{j}Y_{j}^{\top}\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}\Sigma^{1/2}\circ\Sigma^{1/2}\begin{pmatrix}0&X_{k}Y_{k}^{\top}\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}\Sigma^{1/2}\Big)\Big].

Putting together the pieces, we have
\mathbb{E}(T_{\psi_{1}}|\bm{X},\bm{Y})=\sum_{i=1}^{6}T_{i}
.
The proof is now complete by invoking Lemma
E.1
that gives bounds for the variances of
T_{i}
’s.
∎

Lemma E.1
.

Recall the terms
T_{1}
-
T_{6}
defined in the proof of Proposition
10.4
-(1) above. The following hold.

(1)

\var(T_{1})\lesssim_{M}n^{3}\cdot(n\vee\tau_{X}^{2}\vee\tau_{Y}^{2})\cdot(\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})
.

(2)

\var(T_{2})\lesssim_{M}n^{3}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{6}+n^{5}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}
.

(3)

\var(T_{3})\lesssim_{M}n^{3}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{4}\cdot(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{5}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}
.

(4)

\var(T_{4})\vee\var(T_{5})\lesssim_{M}n^{5}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}
.

(5)

\var(T_{6})\lesssim_{M}n^{4}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}
.

(6)

\var(T_{7})\lesssim n^{3}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{5}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}
.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{6}\var(T_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}n^{3}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{4}\cdot(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{5}\cdot(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

Proof of Lemma
E.1
.

(
Variance of
T_{1}
)
By the Poincaré inequality, it is easy to show that
\var\big((X^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X)(Y^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y)\big)\lesssim_{M}(\tau_{X}^{2}\vee\tau_{Y}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}
, so it remains to prove the bound

\displaystyle\var(T_{1})\equiv\var\Big((n-2)\sum_{j,k}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{k})(Y_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{k})\Big)\lesssim_{M}n^{3}(n\vee\tau_{X}^{2}\vee\tau_{Y}^{2})(\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}),

where we slightly abuse the notation
T_{1}
.
Note that

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=(n-2)\cdot\sum_{k,\ell}(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{X}X_{\ell})(Y_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{Y}Y_{\ell})=(n-2)\cdot\sum_{k,\ell}(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[11]}Z_{\ell})(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{\ell}).

Hence for any
i\in[n]
and
j\in[p+q]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial T_{1}}{\partial Z_{ij}}

\displaystyle=2(n-2)\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{n}\Big[(e_{j}^{\top}G_{[11]}Z_{k})(Z_{i}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{k})+(Z_{i}^{\top}G_{[11]}Z_{k})(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[22]}e_{j})\Big]

\displaystyle\equiv 2(n-2)\cdot\big[(T_{11})_{(ij)}+(T_{12})_{(ij)}\big].

By direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\lVert T_{11}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j}\mathbb{E}\bigg[e_{j}^{\top}G_{[11]}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{n}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}\Big)G_{[22]}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{\top}G_{[22]}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{n}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}\Big)G_{[11]}e_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle=n\cdot\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{k_{1}})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{k_{2}})(Z_{k_{1}}^{\top}G_{[11]}^{2}Z_{k_{2}})\Big]

\displaystyle=n\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[1\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2(n-1)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[11]}^{2}Z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(n-1)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{2})(Z_{2}^{\top}G_{[11]}^{2}Z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(n-1)(n-2)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[22]}Z_{3})(Z_{2}^{\top}G_{[11]}^{2}Z_{3})\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim n\cdot\bigg[1\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{X}^{2}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+n\cdot\big[\tr(\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}^{3}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{X}^{3})+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot\tr(\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{X}^{3})\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+n\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}^{2}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{2}\cdot\tr(\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{Y}^{3}\Sigma_{YX}\Sigma_{X}^{3})\bigg]

\displaystyle\asymp_{M}n\cdot\Big[\tau_{Y}^{4}\tau_{X}^{2}+n\cdot\tau_{Y}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n\tau_{X}^{2}\tau_{Y}^{2}+n^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\asymp n\cdot(n\vee\tau_{Y}^{2})\cdot(\tau_{X}^{2}\tau_{Y}^{2}+n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}).

A similar calculation for
T_{12}
yields that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\lVert T_{11}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lesssim_{M}n\cdot(n\vee\tau_{X}^{2})\cdot(\tau_{X}^{2}\tau_{Y}^{2}+n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}),

so we conclude by the Gaussian-Poincaré inequality that

\displaystyle\var(T_{1})\lesssim_{M}n^{3}\cdot(n\vee\tau_{X}^{2}\vee\tau_{Y}^{2})\cdot(\tau_{X}^{2}\tau_{Y}^{2}+n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}).

(
Variance of
T_{2}
)
By the Poincaré inequality, it is easy to show that
\var\big(\sum_{j=1}^{n}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})^{2}\big)
is bounded by the desired order, so we redefine

\displaystyle T_{2}\equiv(n-2)\sum_{j,k}(X_{j}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{k})(X_{k}^{\top}\Sigma_{XY}Y_{j})

with a slight abuse of notation.
Note that

\displaystyle T_{2}=(n-2)\cdot\sum_{k,\ell}(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[12]}Z_{\ell})(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{\ell}).

Hence for any
i\in[n]
and
j\in[p+q]
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial T_{2}}{\partial Z_{ij}}

\displaystyle=2(n-2)\cdot\sum_{k}\Big[(e_{j}^{\top}G_{[12]}Z_{k})(Z_{i}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{k})+(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[12]}e_{j})(Z_{k}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{i})\Big]

\displaystyle\equiv 2(n-2)\cdot\big[(T_{21})_{(ij)}+(T_{22})_{(ij)}\big].

By direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\lVert T_{21}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j}\mathbb{E}\Big[Z_{i}^{\top}G_{[21]}\Big(\sum_{k}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}\Big)G_{[21]}e_{j}e_{j}^{\top}G_{[12]}\Big(\sum_{k}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}\Big)G_{[12]}Z_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle=n\cdot\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}}\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{k_{1}})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{k_{2}})(Z_{k_{1}}^{\top}G_{[21]}G_{[12]}Z_{k_{2}})\Big]

\displaystyle=n\cdot\mathbb{E}\bigg[1\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}G_{[12]}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+2(n-1)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}G_{[12]}Z_{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+(n-1)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{2})(Z_{2}^{\top}G_{[21]}G_{[12]}Z_{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+(n-1)(n-2)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}G_{[21]}Z_{3})(Z_{2}^{\top}G_{[21]}G_{[12]}Z_{3})\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}n\cdot\Big[\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{6}+n\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{2}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\asymp n\cdot\big(\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{6}+n^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\big).

A similar bound holds for
T_{22}
.

(
Variance of
T_{3}
) Note that

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq k}X_{\ell}^{\top}(X_{k}Y_{k}^{\top}-\Sigma_{XY})Y_{\ell}\Big]^{2}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq k}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]^{2}.

Hence for any
i\in[n]
and
j\in[p+q]
, we have

\displaystyle 2^{-1}\frac{\partial T_{3}}{\partial Z_{ij}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq k}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\sum_{\ell\neq k}\Big[\delta_{i\ell}e_{j}^{\top}(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]})Z_{\ell}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\delta_{i\ell}Z_{\ell}^{\top}(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]})e_{j}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\delta_{ik}\cdot Z_{\ell}^{\top}(H_{[11]}e_{j}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}+H_{[11]}Z_{k}e_{j}^{\top}H_{[22]})Z_{\ell}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq i}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]\cdot\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq i}(Z_{\ell}^{\top}H_{[11]}e_{j})(Z_{i}^{\top}H_{[22]}Z_{\ell})\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq i}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]\cdot\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq i}(Z_{\ell}^{\top}H_{[11]}Z_{i})(e_{j}^{\top}H_{[22]}Z_{\ell})\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\neq i}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq k}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]\cdot\Big[e_{j}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\neq i}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq k}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]\cdot\Big[Z_{i}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)e_{j}\Big]

\displaystyle\equiv(T_{31})_{(ij)}+(T_{32})_{(ij)}+(T_{33})_{(ij)}+(T_{34})_{(ij)}.

For
T_{31}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\lVert T_{31}\rVert_{F}^{2}=n\cdot\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq 1}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{1}Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]^{2}\cdot\bigg\lVert\sum_{\ell\neq 1}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}Z_{\ell})H_{[11]}Z_{\ell}\bigg\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq n\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/2}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq 1}Z_{\ell}^{\top}\Big(H_{[11]}Z_{1}Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}\Big)Z_{\ell}\Big]^{4}\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/2}\bigg\lVert\sum_{\ell\neq 1}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}Z_{\ell})H_{[11]}Z_{\ell}\bigg\rVert^{4}.

The two terms can be bounded as follows:

•

Let
U_{1}\equiv H_{[11]}Z_{1}Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}
. By first conditioning on
Z_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{\ell\neq 1}Z_{\ell}^{\top}U_{1}Z_{\ell}\Big]^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\big[n\cdot\tr(U_{1})+n^{1/2}\cdot\lVert U_{1}\rVert_{F}\big]^{4}

(E.3)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}n^{4}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{2}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
E.2
-(4).

•

Let
M_{-1}\equiv\sum_{k\neq 1}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}
. Then by first taking expectation with respect to
Z_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg\lVert\sum_{\ell\neq 1}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}Z_{\ell})H_{[11]}Z_{\ell}\bigg\rVert^{4}\asymp\mathbb{E}\tr^{2}\Big(H_{[22]}M_{-1}H_{[11]}^{2}M_{-1}H_{[22]}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\neq 1}(Z_{k_{1}}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{k_{2}})(Z_{k_{1}}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{k_{2}})\bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\neq 1}(Z_{k_{1}}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{k_{2}})(Z_{k_{1}}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{k_{2}})(Z_{k_{3}}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{k_{4}})(Z_{k_{3}}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{k_{4}}).

(i)

When
k_{1}
-
k_{4}
take one distinct value, there are up to
n
such terms, and each term has contribution

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})^{2}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})^{2}\lesssim\mathbb{E}^{1/2}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})^{4}\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/2}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert H_{[11]}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert H_{[22]}\rVert_{F}^{4}=\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

(ii)

When
k_{1}
-
k_{4}
take two distinct values, there are up to
n^{2}
such terms, and each term has contribution

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{2})\Big]

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/4}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})^{4}\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/4}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})^{4}\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/2}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert H_{[11]}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot\lVert H_{[22]}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot\big[\tr(H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2})+\lVert H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}\rVert_{F}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\cdot(\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}).

(iii)

When
k_{1}
-
k_{4}
take three distinct values in the form of
(k_{1}=k_{2})\neq k_{3}\neq k_{4}
, there are up
n^{3}
such terms, and each term has contribution

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{2}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{3})(Z_{3}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{2})\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1})\Big]\cdot\tr\big(H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\asymp\lVert H_{[11]}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert H_{[22]}\rVert_{F}^{2}\tr\big(H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}\big)\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

(iv)

When
k_{1}
-
k_{4}
take three distinct values in the form of
(k_{1}=k_{3})\neq k_{2}\neq k_{4}
, there are up to
n^{3}
such terms, and each term has contribution

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{3})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{3})\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1}\big)^{2}\asymp_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

(v)

When
k_{1}
-
k_{4}
take four distinct values, there are up to
n^{4}
such terms, and each term has contribution

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{2})(Z_{3}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}Z_{4})(Z_{3}^{\top}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{4})\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathbb{E}Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}Z_{1}\Big]^{2}=\tr^{2}\big(H_{[11]}^{2}H_{[22]}^{2}\big)\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

Putting together estimates (i)-(v) yields that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg\lVert\sum_{\ell\neq 1}(Z_{1}^{\top}H_{[22]}Z_{\ell})H_{[11]}Z_{\ell}\bigg\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}n\cdot\tau_{X}^{4}\tau_{Y}^{4}+n^{2}\cdot\tau_{X}^{2}\tau_{Y}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}+n^{3}\cdot\tau_{X}^{2}\tau_{Y}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{4}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}

(E.4)

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{2}\cdot\tau_{X}^{4}\tau_{Y}^{4}+n^{4}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

The last inequality follows since (1) when the second term dominates the third term
n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}\lesssim 1
, the overall bound is dominated by the first term; (2) when the third term dominates the second term, the overall bound reduces to the sum of the first, third and fourth terms that can be written as a complete square.

Combining (
•
‣
E.2.1
) and (
•
‣
E.2.1
) yields that

(E.5)

\displaystyle T_{31}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}n^{3}\cdot(n\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2})^{2}.

A similar bound holds for
T_{32}
.

Next we bound
T_{33}
. For any
k\in[n]
, let
U_{k}\equiv H_{[11]}Z_{k}Z_{k}^{\top}H_{[22]}-G_{[12]}
be a centered random matrix. Then by definition, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\lVert T_{33}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=n\cdot\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\neq 1}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\sum_{\ell\neq k_{1}}Z_{\ell}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{\ell}\Big)\cdot\Big(\sum_{\ell\neq k_{2}}Z_{\ell}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{\ell}\Big)\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})\Big]

\displaystyle=n\cdot\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}=1}\sum_{k_{1}\notin\{\ell_{1},1\},k_{2}\notin\{\ell_{2},1\}}\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{\ell_{1}}\big)(Z_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{\ell_{2}})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1}).

We now divide the analysis of
T_{33}
into the following four cases:
\ell_{1}=\ell_{2}=1
(Case 1),
\ell_{1}=1
,
\ell_{2}\neq 1
(Case 2),
(\ell_{1}=\ell_{2})\neq 1
(Case 3),
\ell_{1}\neq\ell_{2}\neq 1
(Case 4).

When
\ell_{1}=\ell_{2}=1
,

\displaystyle S_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{k_{1}\neq 1,k_{2}\neq 1}\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\asymp n\cdot\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})^{2}\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})+n^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1}).

(i)

By Lemma
E.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})^{2}\cdot(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/2}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})^{4}\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/2}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\big(\tr(U_{2})+\lVert U_{2}\rVert_{F}\big)^{2}\cdot\lVert U_{2}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lesssim\mathbb{E}\big[\tr(U_{2})^{4}+\lVert U_{2}\rVert_{F}^{4}\big]\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

(ii)

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/4}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1}\big)^{4}\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/4}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})\cdot\mathbb{E}^{1/2}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\big(|\tr(U_{2})|+\lVert U_{2}\rVert_{F}\big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(|\tr(U_{3})|+\lVert U_{2}\rVert_{F}\big)\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(|\tr(U_{2}U_{3})|+\lVert U_{2}U_{3}^{\top}\rVert_{F}\big)

(E.6)

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F})^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}[\lVert U_{2}\rVert_{F}\lVert U_{3}\rVert_{F}]\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

Combining (i) and (ii) yields that in this first case,

(E.7)

\displaystyle S_{1}\lesssim_{M}n^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

•

When
\ell_{1}=1
and
\ell_{2}\neq 1
, we have (by letting
\ell_{2}=2
)

\displaystyle S_{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{k_{1}\neq 1,k_{2}\notin\{1,2\}}\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{1})(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k_{2}\notin\{1,2\}}\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\notin\{1,2\}}\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\asymp n\cdot\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+n\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+n^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{1}).

(i)

Using similar argument as in the previous case, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}.

(ii)

For the third summand, by taking expectation with respect to
Z_{2}
and then
Z_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{1})=\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}\tr(U_{4})U_{4}Z_{1})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3}^{2}\cdot\tr(U_{4})U_{4}^{\top})+\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3}U_{3}^{\top}\tr(U_{4})U_{4})+\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3})\tr(U_{4})\tr(U_{3}^{\top}U_{4})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\tr\Big[\mathbb{E}(U_{3}^{2})\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(\tr(U_{4})U_{4}^{\top}\big)\Big]+\tr\Big[\mathbb{E}(U_{3}U_{3}^{\top})\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(\tr(U_{4})U_{4}\big)\Big]+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

The last line follows from Lemma
E.2
-(6). By Lemma
E.2
-(1)(2)(3), we have

\displaystyle\tr\Big[\mathbb{E}(U_{3}^{2})\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(\tr(U_{4})U_{4}^{\top}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\tr\Big[\Big(G_{[11]}G_{[22]}+\tr(G_{[12]})G_{[12]}+\kappa H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[21]})H_{[22]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\cdot\Big(G_{[22]}G_{[11]}+G_{[21]}^{2}+\kappa H_{[22]}(I\circ G_{[21]})H_{[11]}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\tr(G_{[11]}G_{[22]}G_{[22]}G_{[11]})+\tr(G_{[11]}G_{[22]}G_{[21]}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\tr(G_{[12]})\tr(G_{[12]}G_{[22]}G_{[11]})+\tr(G_{[12]})\tr(G_{[12]}G_{[21]}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa\Big[\tr\big((G_{[11]}G_{[22]}+\tr(G_{[12]})G_{[12]})\cdot H_{[22]}(I\circ G_{[21]})H_{[11]}\big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\tr\big((G_{[22]}G_{[11]}+G_{[21]}^{2})\cdot H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[21]})H_{[22]}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa^{2}\tr\big(H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[21]})H_{[22]}\cdot H_{[22]}(I\circ G_{[21]})H_{[11]}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}),

and

\displaystyle\tr\Big[\mathbb{E}(U_{3}U_{3}^{\top})\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(\tr(U_{4})U_{4}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\tr\Big[\Big(G_{[12]}G_{[21]}+G_{[11]}\tr(G_{[22]})+\kappa H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[22]})H_{[11]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\cdot\Big(G_{[11]}G_{[22]}+G_{[12]}^{2}+\kappa H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[12]})H_{[22]}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\tr(G_{[12]}G_{[21]}G_{[11]}G_{[22]})+\tr(G_{[21]}G_{[12]}^{3})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\tr(G_{[22]})\tr(G_{[11]}^{2}G_{[22]})+\tr(G_{[22]})\tr(G_{[11]}G_{[12]}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa\Big[\tr\big((G_{[12]}G_{[21]}+G_{[11]}\tr(G_{[22]}))\cdot H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[12]})H_{[22]}\big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\tr\big((G_{[11]}G_{[22]}+G_{[12]}^{2})\cdot H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[22]})H_{[11]}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\kappa^{2}\cdot\tr(H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[12]})H_{[22]}\cdot H_{[11]}(I\circ G_{[22]})H_{[11]})

\displaystyle\asymp_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\tr\big[(\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX})^{3}\big]+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\Sigma_{YX}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\asymp_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

Using Lemma
G.5
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}U_{4}^{\top}Z_{1})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}\tr(U_{4})U_{4}^{\top}Z_{1})\lesssim_{M}(\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

Combining (i) and (ii) yields that in this second case,

(E.8)

\displaystyle S_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}n\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{2}(\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

A similar estimate holds for the case

.

When
(\ell_{1}=\ell_{2})\neq 1
, we have (letting

\displaystyle S_{3}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\notin\{1,2\}}\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\asymp n\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2}\big)^{2}(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{1})+n^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{1})

\displaystyle=n\cdot\mathbb{E}(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2})^{2}\lVert U_{3}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{2}\mathbb{E}(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2})(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2})\tr(U_{3}^{\top}U_{4})

\displaystyle\asymp n\cdot\big[\mathbb{E}\lVert U_{3}\rVert_{F}^{4}+\mathbb{E}\tr^{2}(U_{3})\lVert U_{3}\rVert_{F}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+n^{2}\cdot\Big[\mathbb{E}\tr^{2}(U_{3}U_{4}^{\top})+\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3}U_{4})\tr(U_{3}U_{4}^{\top})+\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3})\tr(U_{4})\tr(U_{3}^{\top}U_{4})\Big].

,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\lVert U_{3}\rVert_{F}^{4}+\mathbb{E}\tr^{2}(U_{3})\lVert U_{3}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\asymp_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\tr^{2}(U_{3}U_{4}^{\top})+\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3}U_{4})\tr(U_{3}U_{4}^{\top})+\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3})\tr(U_{4})\tr(U_{3}^{\top}U_{4})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\asymp\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

Consequently,

(E.9)

\displaystyle S_{3}

\displaystyle\asymp_{M}n\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

When

and

\displaystyle S_{4}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{k_{1}\notin\{1,2\},k_{2}\notin\{1,3\}}\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{3}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{3})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{k_{1}}^{\top}U_{k_{2}}Z_{1})

\displaystyle=\Big[\sum_{k_{1}=3,k_{2}=2}+\sum_{k_{1}=3,k_{2}\notin\{1,2,3\}}+\sum_{k_{1}\notin\{1,2,3\},k_{2}=2}+\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\notin\{1,2,3\}}\Big](\cdots)

\displaystyle\asymp\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{3}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{3})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})+n\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{3}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{3}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{3})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{3}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+n\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{3}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{3})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{4}^{\top}U_{2}Z_{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+n^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\big(Z_{2}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{2}\big)(Z_{3}^{\top}U_{5}Z_{3})(Z_{1}^{\top}U_{4}^{\top}U_{5}Z_{1})\equiv\sum_{\ell}S_{4,\ell}.

(i)

By (

),

.

-(4)(5)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\tr(U_{3})(Z_{3}^{\top}U_{4}Z_{3})\tr(U_{3}^{\top}U_{4})

(E.10)

\displaystyle S_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n\cdot(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{2}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

\displaystyle\quad\quad+n^{2}\cdot\big[n\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{2}(\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}+\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+n^{2}\cdot\big[n\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+n^{3}\cdot\big[\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n\cdot(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{2}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\asymp n^{3}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{4}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{4}+n^{4}\cdot(1\vee\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2})\lVert\Sigma_{X}\rVert_{F}^{2}\lVert\Sigma_{Y}\rVert_{F}^{2}+n^{5}\cdot\lVert\Sigma_{XY}\rVert_{F}^{2}.

A similar bound holds for
T_{34}
. The above bound dominates the bound (

) for

and

and hence is a valid bound for
